# MCP Tools Reference

_Generated by `pnpm docs:tools` from the live catalog — do not edit by hand._

**342 tools**: 325 supported, 2 require an external AI provider, 15 unsupported (return `UNSUPPORTED` with an alternative).

## Common output schema

Every tool returns one text content block containing JSON, also sent as `structuredContent`:

```json
{ "ok": true, "tool": "<name>", "source": "resolve | mock | server", "status": "ok", "message": "...", "data": { "...tool specific..." }, "warnings": ["..."] }
```

Errors set `isError: true` and return `{ "ok": false, "tool": "<name>", "status": "error", "message": "...", "error": { "code": "OPERATION_FAILED", "message": "...", "details": {}, "hint": "..." } }`.
`status` is one of ok, partial, unsupported, error, confirmation_required.
Codes: VALIDATION_ERROR, CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED, RESOLVE_NOT_INSTALLED, RESOLVE_NOT_RUNNING, SCRIPTING_UNAVAILABLE, PYTHON_NOT_FOUND, NO_PROJECT, NO_TIMELINE, NOT_FOUND, AMBIGUOUS, ALREADY_EXISTS, PATH_NOT_ALLOWED, FILE_EXISTS, OPERATION_FAILED, UNSUPPORTED, TIMEOUT, BRIDGE_BUSY, BRIDGE_CRASHED, UNKNOWN_OP, RENDER_FAILED, PROVIDER_NOT_CONFIGURED, PROVIDER_ERROR.

**Addressing.** Timeline items are addressed by `itemId` (from `timeline_get_clips`) or `trackType` + `trackIndex` + `itemIndex` (1-based, clips only). Frames are integers; absolute timeline frames include the timeline start.

**Permissions.** Read-only tools need nothing beyond a reachable DaVinci Resolve (external scripting set to Local). Write tools need an open project (unless they create one). Tools that write files need the path inside `ALLOWED_WRITE_PATHS`. Destructive tools need `confirm: true`. AI-provider tools need provider credentials and network access.


## 1. Application

### `resolve_get_status`

Check whether DaVinci Resolve is installed and running, whether scripting is connected, and the open project/timeline/page. Call this first.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {},
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"resolve_get_status","arguments":{}}`

### `resolve_check_scripting_connection`

Verify the scripting API connection end-to-end (Python bridge, fusionscript, Resolve) and report exactly which layer fails.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {},
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"resolve_check_scripting_connection","arguments":{}}`

### `resolve_launch`

Launch DaVinci Resolve if it is not running and wait (up to waitSeconds) for the scripting API to accept connections.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "wait": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "waitSeconds": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 10,
      "maximum": 600,
      "default": 180
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"resolve_launch","arguments":{}}`

### `resolve_close`

Quit DaVinci Resolve. Unsaved changes may be lost unless saveProject is true. DESTRUCTIVE: requires confirm: true after explicit user confirmation.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** destructive (confirm: true)
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "saveProject": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "confirm": {
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"resolve_close","arguments":{}}`

## 17. Background jobs & meta

### `get_job`

Status of a background job (queued | running | succeeded | failed | cancelled). When it has succeeded, also returns the tool's full result.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Implementation:** server-side planner / workflow

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "jobId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 64,
      "description": "Id returned by a tool called with background: true"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "jobId"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"get_job","arguments":{"jobId":"Example"}}`

### `wait_for_job`

Wait up to timeoutSeconds for a background job to finish, then return it as get_job does. If it is still running, call again; the job keeps going either way.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Implementation:** server-side planner / workflow

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "jobId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 64,
      "description": "Id returned by a tool called with background: true"
    },
    "timeoutSeconds": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 55,
      "default": 30
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "jobId"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"wait_for_job","arguments":{"jobId":"Example"}}`

### `list_jobs`

List recent background jobs, newest first (results omitted; use get_job).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Implementation:** server-side planner / workflow

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {},
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"list_jobs","arguments":{}}`

### `cancel_job`

Cancel a queued background job. A job already running inside DaVinci Resolve cannot be interrupted; the result says so (use render_cancel for renders).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** server-side planner / workflow

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "jobId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 64,
      "description": "Id returned by a tool called with background: true"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "jobId"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"cancel_job","arguments":{"jobId":"Example"}}`

## 2. Projects

### `project_create`

Create a new project in the current project-manager folder (and open it). Optionally set resolution, frame rate and color science, e.g. 4K 24 fps.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 255,
      "pattern": "^[^\\\\/:*?\"<>|]+$"
    },
    "width": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 16,
      "maximum": 16384
    },
    "height": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 16,
      "maximum": 16384
    },
    "frameRate": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "23.976",
        "24",
        "25",
        "29.97",
        "30",
        "47.952",
        "48",
        "50",
        "59.94",
        "60",
        "119.88",
        "120"
      ]
    },
    "playbackFrameRate": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "23.976",
        "24",
        "25",
        "29.97",
        "30",
        "47.952",
        "48",
        "50",
        "59.94",
        "60",
        "119.88",
        "120"
      ]
    },
    "colorScienceMode": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "colorSpaceTimeline": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "colorSpaceOutput": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "raw": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {
        "anyOf": [
          {
            "type": "string"
          },
          {
            "type": "number"
          },
          {
            "type": "boolean"
          }
        ]
      }
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "name"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"project_create","arguments":{"name":"Example"}}`

### `project_save_as`

Save the current project under a new name. Resolve has no direct Save-As API; this exports the project to a .drp and imports it under the new name, then optionally opens it.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "newName": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 255,
      "pattern": "^[^\\\\/:*?\"<>|]+$"
    },
    "open": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "newName"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"project_save_as","arguments":{"newName":"Example"}}`

### `project_close`

Close the current project (saving first unless save=false).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "save": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"project_close","arguments":{}}`

### `project_delete`

Delete a project from the database. A .drp backup is exported first (BACKUP_BEFORE_DESTRUCTIVE). The project must not be open. DESTRUCTIVE: requires confirm: true after explicit user confirmation.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** destructive (confirm: true)
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 255,
      "pattern": "^[^\\\\/:*?\"<>|]+$"
    },
    "confirm": {
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "name"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"project_delete","arguments":{"name":"Example"}}`

```json
        "59.94",
        "60",
        "119.88",
        "120"

      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "23.976",
        "24",
        "25",
        "29.97",
        "30",
        "47.952",
        "48",
        "50",
        "59.94",
        "60",
        "119.88",
        "120"
      ]
    },
    "colorScienceMode": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "colorSpaceTimeline": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "colorSpaceOutput": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "raw": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {
        "anyOf": [
          {
            "type": "string"
          },

            "type": "number"
```

### `project_backup`

Export a project (default: current) to a timestamped .drp in BACKUP_DIR (or a given folder under the allowed roots).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 255,
      "pattern": "^[^\\\\/:*?\"<>|]+$"
    },
    "folder": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 3,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Absolute local file path"
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"project_backup","arguments":{}}`

### `project_restore`

Restore a project from a .drp (import) or .dra (archive restore) under a new name. Never overwrites an existing project.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "path": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 3,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Absolute local file path"
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 255,
      "pattern": "^[^\\\\/:*?\"<>|]+$",
      "description": "Name for the restored project (must not already exist)"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "path",
    "name"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"project_restore","arguments":{"path":"C:\\Users\\you\\Videos\\example.mp4","name":"Example"}}`

## 3. Media

### `media_import`

Import any supported media files (video, audio, stills, subtitles) into the media pool, optionally into a bin.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "paths": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 3,
        "maxLength": 1024,
        "description": "Absolute local file path"
      }
    },
    "bin": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 512
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "paths"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"media_import","arguments":{"paths":[]}}`

### `media_import_folder`

Import all supported media from a folder (recursive by default). organizeByType puts files into Video/Audio/Images bins.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "path": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 3,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Absolute local file path"
    },
    "recursive": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "types": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "enum": [
          "video",
          "audio",
          "image"
        ]
      }
    },
    "organizeByType": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "bin": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 512
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "path"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"media_import_folder","arguments":{"path":"C:\\Users\\you\\Videos\\example.mp4"}}`

### `media_import_sequence`

Import an image sequence as one clip. pattern uses printf-style frame numbering, e.g. C:\renders\shot_%04d.exr.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "pattern": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 3,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "pattern": "%0?\\d*d",
      "description": "Absolute local file path"
    },
    "start": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "end": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "bin": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 512
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "pattern",
    "start",
    "end"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"media_import_sequence","arguments":{"pattern":"Example","start":1,"end":1}}`

Remove clips from the media pool. Files on disk are NOT deleted. Timeline uses of the clips go offline. DESTRUCTIVE: requires confirm: true after explicit user confirmation.

```json

      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 1024,
        "description": "Media pool clip id, exact clip name, or file path"
      }
    },
    "confirm": {
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "clips"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

### `media_find_duplicates`

Find duplicate source files among media pool clips. 'quick' compares size+name, 'hash' compares SHA-256 (reads every file).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "method": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "quick",
        "hash"
      ],
      "default": "quick"
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"media_find_duplicates","arguments":{}}`

## 4. Timeline

### `timeline_create`

Create a timeline (empty, or from media pool clips in order) and make it current. Width/height/frameRate apply custom timeline settings (e.g. 1080x1920 vertical).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 255
    },
    "clips": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 1024,
        "description": "Media pool clip id, exact clip name, or file path"
      }
    },
    "width": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 16,
      "maximum": 16384
    },
    "height": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 16,
      "maximum": 16384
    },
    "frameRate": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "23.976",
        "24",
        "25",
        "29.97",
        "30",
        "48",
        "50",
        "59.94",
        "60"
      ]
    },
    "raw": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {
        "anyOf": [
          {
            "type": "string"
          },
          {
            "type": "number"
          }
        ]
      }
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "name"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"timeline_create","arguments":{"name":"Example"}}`

Delete a timeline from the project. DESTRUCTIVE: requires confirm: true after explicit user confirmation.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** destructive (confirm: true)

```json

      "type": "string",

    },
    "confirm": {
      "type": "boolean"

  "required": [
    "name"

  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"timeline_delete","arguments":{"name":"Example"}}`

### `timeline_add_track`

Add a video, audio or subtitle track. Audio tracks take a channel format (mono, stereo, 5.1, 7.1, adaptive1..24).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "trackType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "video",
        "audio",
        "subtitle"
      ]
    },
    "audioType": {
      "type": "string",
      "default": "stereo"
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "trackType"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

### `timeline_set_start_timecode`

Set the timeline start timecode, e.g. 01:00:00:00.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "timecode": {
      "type": "string",

    }

  "required": [

  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

### `timeline_export_edl`

Export the timeline as an EDL (CMX 3600). The output path must be under an allowed write folder; existing files need overwrite: true.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "path": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 3,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Absolute local file path"
    },
    "format": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "edl",
        "edl_cdl",
        "edl_missing"
      ],
      "default": "edl"
    },
    "overwrite": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "path"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"timeline_export_edl","arguments":{"path":"C:\\Users\\you\\Videos\\example.mp4"}}`

```json
        "aaf_new",
        "aaf_existing"
      ],
      "default": "aaf_new"
    },
    "overwrite": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "path"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"timeline_export_aaf","arguments":{"path":"C:\\Users\\you\\Videos\\example.mp4"}}`

### `timeline_import`

Import a timeline from AAF/EDL/XML/FCPXML/DRP/OTIO into the media pool.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "path": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 3,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Absolute local file path"
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "importSourceClips": {
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "path"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"timeline_import","arguments":{"path":"C:\\Users\\you\\Videos\\example.mp4"}}`

Add a marker on the timeline at a frame offset from the timeline start.

```json
{
  "type": "object",

        "Pink",
        "Purple",
        "Fuchsia",
        "Rose",
        "Lavender",
        "Sky",
        "Mint",
        "Lemon",
        "Sand",
        "Cocoa",
        "Cream"

      "default": "Blue"

    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "default": ""
    },
    "note": {
      "type": "string",
      "default": ""
    },
    "duration": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "frame"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

## 5. Editing

### `clip_append`

Append media pool clips to the end of the current timeline (in order), optionally with source ranges and target tracks.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "clips": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "clip": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 1024,
            "description": "Media pool clip id, exact clip name, or file path"
          },
          "sourceStart": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 0
          },
          "sourceEnd": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 0
          },
          "trackIndex": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1
          },
          "recordFrame": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 0
          },
          "mediaType": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "video",
              "audio"
            ]
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "clip"
        ],
        "additionalProperties": false
      }
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "clips"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"clip_append","arguments":{"clips":[]}}`

### `clip_add`

Place one media pool clip on the timeline at a given record frame and track (AppendToTimeline with recordFrame).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "clip": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Media pool clip id, exact clip name, or file path"
    },
    "sourceStart": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "sourceEnd": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    },
    "recordFrame": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "mediaType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "video",
        "audio"
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "clip"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"clip_add","arguments":{"clip":"Example"}}`

### `clip_insert`

Place a clip at a record frame on a track that is empty there. The API has no ripple insert: to make room, move later clips first (clip_move) or use an empty track.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "clip": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Media pool clip id, exact clip name, or file path"
    },
    "sourceStart": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "sourceEnd": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    },
    "recordFrame": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "mediaType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "video",
        "audio"
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "clip",
    "recordFrame"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"clip_insert","arguments":{"clip":"Example","recordFrame":1}}`

### `clip_overwrite`

Overwrite the timeline range [recordFrame, recordFrame+length) on a track with a clip: overlapping items on that track are removed first, then the clip is placed. DESTRUCTIVE: requires confirm: true after explicit user confirmation.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** destructive (confirm: true)
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "clip": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Media pool clip id, exact clip name, or file path"
    },
    "sourceStart": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "sourceEnd": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "recordFrame": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "mediaType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "video",
        "audio"
      ]
    },
    "length": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    },
    "confirm": {
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "clip",
    "recordFrame",
    "length"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"clip_overwrite","arguments":{"clip":"Example","recordFrame":1,"length":1}}`

Replace a timeline item with a different media pool clip at the same position and length. DESTRUCTIVE: requires confirm: true after explicit user confirmation.

```json
    },

      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },

      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },

      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,

      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "default": 0
    },
    "confirm": {
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "clip"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

### `clip_split`

Split a timeline item at an absolute timeline frame into two items. Implemented as a re-placement (remove item, append source range again) because the API has no direct trim/move/split: grades, effects and transforms on the original item are not carried over. DESTRUCTIVE: requires confirm: true after explicit user confirmation.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** destructive (confirm: true)
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "itemId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "trackType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "video",
        "audio",
        "subtitle"
      ],
      "default": "video"
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "itemIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "frame": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "confirm": {
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "frame"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"clip_split","arguments":{"frame":1}}`

```json
}
```

```json
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1,
            "default": 1
          },
          "itemIndex": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1,
            "default": 1
          }

        "required": [],
        "additionalProperties": false
      }
```

```json
      "additionalProperties": {

      }
    }

}
```

```json
}
```

### `clip_freeze_frame`

Freeze frame. engine 'resolve' (default) sets clip speed to 0% (SetSpeed, Resolve 21+: holds the first frame); engine 'fusion' holds a chosen composition frame with a TimeStretcher.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json

  "type": "object",

      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "itemId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "trackType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "video",
        "audio",
        "subtitle"
      ],
      "default": "video"
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "itemIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "engine": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "resolve",
        "fusion"
      ],
      "default": "resolve"
    },
    "frame": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "default": 0
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"clip_freeze_frame","arguments":{}}`

### `clip_set_speed`

Change clip speed (0.5 = 50% slow motion, 2 = 200%). engine 'resolve' (default) uses TimelineItem.SetSpeed (Resolve 21+; item length changes, ripple optional); engine 'fusion' retimes inside the clip's Fusion composition without changing its length.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "itemId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "trackType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "video",
        "audio",
        "subtitle"
      ],
      "default": "video"
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "itemIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "speed": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0.01,
      "maximum": 100
    },
    "engine": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "resolve",
        "fusion"
      ],
      "default": "resolve"
    },
    "ripple": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "pitchCorrection": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "interpolate": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "speed"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"clip_set_speed","arguments":{"speed":0.01}}`

Insert a generator at the playhead (standard e.g. 'Solid Color', '10 Step'; fusion; or OFX by name).

## 6. Effects & transitions

### `effect_list`

List effects installed in this Resolve (Resolve.GetEffectsList: ResolveFX/OFX and Fusion effects; mediaType video|audio|photo). With fusionNodes: true also lists the Fusion node aliases usable with engine 'fusion'.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {

      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "video",
        "audio",
        "photo"
      ]
    },
    "category": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "ofx",
        "fusion",
        "audio"
      ]
    },
    "query": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "fusionNodes": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "registry": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

### `effect_apply`

Apply an effect to a timeline item. engine 'resolve' (default) uses TimelineItem.AddEffect with an installed effect name/id (e.g. 'Gaussian Blur', 'Film Grain', 'Vignette', 'Lens Flare', or an alias like blur/film_grain/vignette). engine 'fusion' inserts a Fusion node into the item's composition (parameters are then settable with effect_set_parameters).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",

        "subtitle"

      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },

      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "effect": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1
    },

      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "resolve",
        "fusion"
      ],
      "default": "resolve"

    "category": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "ofx",
        "fusion",
        "audio"
      ]
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "parameters": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {}

  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

### `transition_add`

Add a transition at the start or end of a timeline item (TimelineItem.AddTransition, Resolve 21+). E.g. Cross Dissolve, Dip To Color Dissolve, Push, Slide, Zoom (fusion), Cross Fade +3 dB (audio). Needs media handles at the edit.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "itemId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "trackType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "video",
        "audio",
        "subtitle"
      ],
      "default": "video"
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "itemIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "type": {
      "type": "string",
      "default": "Cross Dissolve"
    },
    "category": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "simple",
        "fusion",
        "ofx",
        "audio"
      ],
      "default": "simple"
    },
    "position": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "start",
        "end"
      ],
      "default": "end"
    },
    "alignment": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "left",
        "center",
        "right"
      ],
      "default": "center"
    },
    "duration": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 1000
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"transition_add","arguments":{}}`

## 7. Speed

### `speed_set`

Constant clip speed in percent (e.g. 50 = slow motion, 200 = fast). engine 'resolve' (default): TimelineItem.SetSpeed (Resolve 21+; changes item length; ripple optional). engine 'fusion': Fusion-based retiming remaps frames inside the clip's composition; the item's length on the timeline is not changed (the API has no speed-change/ripple). For true retime with length change use the Edit page Change Clip Speed.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "itemId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "trackType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "video",
        "audio",
        "subtitle"
      ],
      "default": "video"
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "itemIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "percentage": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 10000
    },
    "engine": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "resolve",
        "fusion"
      ],
      "default": "resolve"
    },
    "ripple": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "pitchCorrection": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "stretchKeyframes": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "interpolate": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "percentage"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"speed_set","arguments":{"percentage":1}}`

### `speed_ramp_create`

Speed ramp from keyframes mapping composition frames to source frames (e.g. normal -> slow -> normal). Fusion-based retiming remaps frames inside the clip's composition; the item's length on the timeline is not changed (the API has no speed-change/ripple). For true retime with length change use the Edit page Change Clip Speed.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Fusion scripting

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "itemId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "trackType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "video",
        "audio",
        "subtitle"
      ],
      "default": "video"
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "itemIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "keys": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "frame": {
            "type": "number",
            "minimum": 0,
            "description": "Composition frame"
          },
          "sourceFrame": {
```

### `speed_reverse`

Reverse playback. Fusion-based retiming remaps frames inside the clip's composition; the item's length on the timeline is not changed (the API has no speed-change/ripple). For true retime with length change use the Edit page Change Clip Speed.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Fusion scripting

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {

      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "itemId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "trackType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "video",
        "audio",
        "subtitle"
      ],
      "default": "video"
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "itemIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

## 8. Titles

### `title_create`

Add a title. mode 'overlay' (default, non-destructive): Text+ composited over the video clip under the given frame, inside that clip's Fusion composition (lasts the clip's length). mode 'insert': Resolve's InsertFusionTitleIntoTimeline/InsertTitleIntoTimeline at the playhead — verified on Resolve 21 to RIPPLE-INSERT on the destination track (pushes later clips), so it needs confirm: true.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Fusion scripting

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "timecode": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "frame": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "mode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "overlay",
        "insert"
      ],
      "default": "overlay"
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    },
    "text": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 5000,
      "default": "Title"
    },
    "kind": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "fusion",
        "standard"
      ],
      "default": "fusion"
    },
    "template": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "style": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "text": {
          "type": "string",
          "maxLength": 5000
        },
        "font": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "fontStyle": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "size": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 1
        },
        "color": {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^#?[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$",
          "description": "Hex color like #ff8800"
        },
        "opacity": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 1
        },
        "position": {
          "type": "array",
          "prefixItems": [
            {
              "type": "number"
            },
            {
              "type": "number"
            }
          ]
        },
        "alignment": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "left",
            "center",
            "right"
          ]
        },
        "tracking": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "lineSpacing": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "background": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "color": {
              "type": "string",
              "pattern": "^#?[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$",
              "description": "Hex color like #ff8800",
              "default": "#000000"
            },
            "opacity": {
              "type": "number",
              "minimum": 0,
              "maximum": 1,
              "default": 0.75
            }
          },
          "required": [],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        "outline": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "color": {
              "type": "string",
              "pattern": "^#?[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$",
              "description": "Hex color like #ff8800",
              "default": "#000000"
            },
            "thickness": {
              "type": "number",
              "minimum": 0,
              "maximum": 0.5,
              "default": 0.02
            }
          },
          "required": [],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        "shadow": {
          "type": "boolean"
        }
      },
      "required": [],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "animation": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "type": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "fade",
            "slide_up",
            "zoom",
            "scroll",
            "typewriter"
          ]
        },
        "frames": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 1,
          "maximum": 10000,
          "default": 15
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "type"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "confirm": {
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"title_create","arguments":{}}`

```json
    "itemId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "trackType": {
      "type": "string",

        "video",
        "audio",
        "subtitle"
      ],
      "default": "video"
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "itemIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "compIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "color": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^#?[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$",

      "default": "#000000"

      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 1,
      "default": 0.75
    }

}
```

### `title_set_animation`

Animate a Text+ title (fade, slide_up, zoom, scroll, typewriter) with Fusion keyframes.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Fusion scripting

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "itemId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "trackType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "video",
        "audio",
        "subtitle"
      ],
      "default": "video"
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "itemIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "compIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "animation": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "type": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "fade",
            "slide_up",
            "zoom",
            "scroll",
            "typewriter"
          ]
        },
        "frames": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 1,
          "maximum": 10000,
          "default": 15
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "type"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "animation"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"title_set_animation","arguments":{"animation":{}}}`

```json
}
```

## 9. Subtitles

### `subtitle_create_track`

Add a subtitle track to the timeline.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"subtitle_create_track","arguments":{}}`

### `subtitle_import`

Import an SRT (or other Resolve-supported subtitle file) and place it on the timeline's subtitle track.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "path": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 3,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Absolute local file path"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "path"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"subtitle_import","arguments":{"path":"C:\\Users\\you\\Videos\\example.mp4"}}`

### `subtitle_export`

Export the timeline's subtitle items to an SRT file (built from the subtitle track contents).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "path": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 3,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Absolute local file path"
    },
    "track": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    },
    "overwrite": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "path"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"subtitle_export","arguments":{"path":"C:\\Users\\you\\Videos\\example.mp4"}}`

```json
    "text": {
      "type": "string"
    }
```

### `subtitle_generate`

Generate subtitles from the timeline audio. engine 'resolve' uses Resolve Studio's CreateSubtitlesFromAudio; engine 'provider' transcribes a rendered/source audio file with the configured AI provider and imports the SRT.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "engine": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "resolve",
        "provider"
      ],
      "default": "resolve"
    },
    "language": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "charsPerLine": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 60
    },
    "lines": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "const": 1
        },
        {
          "const": 2
        }
      ]
    },
    "gapFrames": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 10
    },
    "audioPath": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 3,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Absolute local file path"
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"subtitle_generate","arguments":{}}`

## 10. Fusion

### `fusion_get_composition`

Inspect a Fusion composition (item's comp, or the Fusion page's current comp): name, render range, nodes. create: true adds a comp to the item if it has none.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Implementation:** Fusion scripting

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "itemId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "trackType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "video",
        "audio",
        "subtitle"
      ],
      "default": "video"
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "itemIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "source": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "item",
        "current"
      ],
      "default": "item"
    },
    "compIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "create": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

### `fusion_create_node`

Create any Fusion node by RegID or alias (TextPlus, Background, Merge, Transform, Blur, Glow, Camera3D, Shape3D, pEmitter...). The type is validated by Fusion; unknown types are reported. insertIntoChain puts it before MediaOut.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Fusion scripting

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "itemId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "trackType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "video",
        "audio",
        "subtitle"
      ],
      "default": "video"
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "itemIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "source": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "item",
        "current"
      ],
      "default": "item"
    },
    "compIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "type": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 128,
      "pattern": "^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$"
    },
    "inputs": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {},
      "description": "Fusion input id -> value (numbers, strings, [x,y] points)"
    },
    "insertIntoChain": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "x": {
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "y": {
      "type": "integer"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "type"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"fusion_create_node","arguments":{"type":"Example"}}`

### `fusion_create_text`

Create a Text+ node with text, font, size and color, merged over the clip (or output directly when overClip is false).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Fusion scripting

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "itemId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "trackType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "video",
        "audio",
        "subtitle"
      ],
      "default": "video"
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "itemIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "source": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "item",
        "current"
      ],
      "default": "item"
    },
    "compIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "text": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 5000
    },
    "font": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "size": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 1,
      "default": 0.08
    },
    "color": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^#?[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$",
      "description": "Hex color like #ff8800",
      "default": "#ffffff"
    },
    "position": {
      "type": "array",
      "prefixItems": [
        {
          "type": "number"
        },
        {
          "type": "number"
        }
      ],
      "default": [
        0.5,
        0.5
      ]
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 128,
      "pattern": "^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$",
      "default": "MCP_Text"
    },
    "overClip": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "text"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"fusion_create_text","arguments":{"text":"Example"}}`

### `fusion_build_graph`

Build a whole node graph from a declarative spec: nodes [{name,type,inputs}], connections [{from,to,input}], keyframes [{node,input,keys}], output node. All types/inputs validated; errors listed per node.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Fusion scripting

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "itemId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "trackType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "video",
        "audio",
        "subtitle"
      ],
      "default": "video"
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "itemIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "source": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "item",
        "current"
      ],
      "default": "item"
    },
    "compIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "nodes": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "name": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 128,
            "pattern": "^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$"
          },
          "type": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "inputs": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": {},
            "description": "Fusion input id -> value (numbers, strings, [x,y] points)"
          },
          "x": {
            "type": "integer"
          },
          "y": {
            "type": "integer"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "name",
          "type"
        ],
        "additionalProperties": false
      }
    },
    "connections": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "from": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "to": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "input": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "from",
          "to"
        ],
        "additionalProperties": false
      },
      "default": []
    },
    "keyframes": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "node": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "input": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "keys": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": {
              "type": "object",
              "properties": {
                "frame": {
                  "type": "number"
                },
                "value": {
                  "anyOf": [
                    {
                      "type": "number"
                    },
                    {
                      "type": "string"
                    },
                    {
                      "type": "array",
                      "prefixItems": [
                        {
                          "type": "number"
                        },
                        {
                          "type": "number"
                        }
                      ]
                    },
                    {
                      "type": "array",
                      "items": {
                        "type": "number"
                      }
                    }
                  ]
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "frame",
                "value"
              ],
              "additionalProperties": false
            }
          },
          "path": {
            "type": "boolean"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "node",
          "input",
          "keys"
        ],
        "additionalProperties": false
      },
      "default": []
    },
    "output": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "nodes"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

### `color_set_cdl`

Apply an ASC CDL to a node: slope (≈ gain), offset, power (≈ inverse gamma), saturation. This is the scriptable primary correction.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "itemId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "trackType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "video",
        "audio",
        "subtitle"
      ],
      "default": "video"
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "itemIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "nodeIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "slope": {
      "type": "array",
      "prefixItems": [
        {
          "type": "number"
        },
        {
          "type": "number"
        },
        {
          "type": "number"
        }
      ],
      "default": [
        1,
        1,
        1
      ]
    },
    "offset": {
      "type": "array",
      "prefixItems": [
        {
          "type": "number"
        },
        {
          "type": "number"
        },
        {
          "type": "number"
        }
      ],
      "default": [
        0,
        0,
        0
      ]
    },
    "power": {
      "type": "array",
      "prefixItems": [
        {
          "type": "number"
        },
        {
          "type": "number"
        },
        {
          "type": "number"
        }
      ],
      "default": [
        1,
        1,
        1
      ]
    },
    "saturation": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 4,
      "default": 1
    },
    "preserve": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"color_set_cdl","arguments":{}}`

- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

### `color_create_lut`

Generate a 3D .cube LUT from look parameters (exposure, white balance, lift/gamma/gain, contrast, saturation, split toning, fade, monochrome) or a named preset.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "path": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 3,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Absolute local file path"
    },
    "preset": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "hollywood_cinematic",
        "teal_orange",
        "filmic_warm",
        "vintage_film",
        "black_and_white",
        "moody_cinematic",
        "bright_commercial",
        "natural_documentary",
        "desaturated_cinematic",
        "night_cinematic"
      ]
    },
    "params": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "exposure": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": -5,
          "maximum": 5
        },
        "temperature": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": -1,
          "maximum": 1
        },
        "tint": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": -1,
          "maximum": 1
        },
        "lift": {
          "type": "array",
          "prefixItems": [
            {
              "type": "number"
            },
            {
              "type": "number"
            },
            {
              "type": "number"
            }
          ]
        },
        "gamma": {
          "type": "array",
          "prefixItems": [
            {
              "type": "number"
            },
            {
              "type": "number"
            },
            {
              "type": "number"
            }
          ]
        },
        "gain": {
          "type": "array",
          "prefixItems": [
            {
              "type": "number"
            },
            {
              "type": "number"
            },
            {
              "type": "number"
            }
          ]
        },
        "contrast": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 3
        },
        "pivot": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 1
        },
        "saturation": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 3
        },
        "vibrance": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": -1,
          "maximum": 1
        },
        "shadowsTint": {
          "type": "array",
          "prefixItems": [
            {
              "type": "number"
            },
            {
              "type": "number"
            },
            {
              "type": "number"
            }
          ]
        },
        "highlightsTint": {
          "type": "array",
          "prefixItems": [
            {
              "type": "number"
            },
            {
              "type": "number"
            },
            {
              "type": "number"
            }
          ]
        },
        "fade": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 0.5
        },
        "monochrome": {
          "type": "boolean"
        }
      },
      "required": [],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "size": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 17,
      "maximum": 65,
      "default": 33
    },
    "overwrite": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "path"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"color_create_lut","arguments":{"path":"C:\\Users\\you\\Videos\\example.mp4"}}`

```json
      "default": 1
    },

      "type": "integer",
```

```json
      "maximum": 24,

      "maximum": 100,
      "default": 0

}
```

### `audio_dialogue_leveler`

Enable the Dialogue Leveler on a clip (reduce loud / lift soft dialogue, background reduction). Attempted via TimelineItem.SetProperty and verified by readback; reports 'unsupported' when this Resolve version rejects the property.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "itemId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "trackType": {
      "const": "audio",
      "default": "audio"
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "itemIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "enabled": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "reduceLoud": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "liftSoft": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "backgroundReduction": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "outputGainDb": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": -24,
      "maximum": 24,
      "default": 0
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"audio_dialogue_leveler","arguments":{}}`

### `audio_master`

Mastering loudness: normalize audio items to a delivery standard (Timeline.NormalizeAudioLevel, Resolve 21+). Modes include EBU R128, ATSC A/85, Netflix, YouTube, TR-B32, Sample Peak Program, True Peak.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "mode": {
      "type": "string",
      "default": "YouTube"
    },
    "targetLoudness": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": -60,
      "maximum": 0
    },
    "targetLevel": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": -60,
      "maximum": 0
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    },
    "items": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "itemId": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "trackType": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "video",
              "audio",
              "subtitle"
            ],
            "default": "video"
          },
          "trackIndex": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1,
            "default": 1
          },
          "itemIndex": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1,
            "default": 1
          }
        },
        "required": [],
        "additionalProperties": false
      }
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"audio_master","arguments":{}}`

### `audio_dialogue_cleanup`

Dialogue cleanup on an audio clip: Voice Isolation (amount 0-100) plus the Dialogue Leveler via item properties (Resolve 21+). Falls back to track-level Voice Isolation when itemIndex is omitted.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "itemId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "trackType": {
      "const": "audio",
      "default": "audio"
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "itemIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "amount": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 100,
      "default": 60
    },
    "leveler": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "wholeTrack": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

### `audio_mix`

Balance a mix by setting per-item volume (dB) for several audio items in one call (e.g. dialogue 0 dB, music -18 dB). Each change is verified; unsupported properties are reported.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "levels": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "item": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "itemId": {
                "type": "string"
              },
              "trackType": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": [
                  "video",
                  "audio",
                  "subtitle"
                ],
                "default": "video"
              },
              "trackIndex": {
                "type": "integer",
                "minimum": 1,
                "default": 1
              },
              "itemIndex": {
                "type": "integer",
                "minimum": 1,
                "default": 1
              }
            },
            "required": [],
            "additionalProperties": false
          },
          "volumeDb": {
            "type": "number",
            "minimum": -100,
            "maximum": 24
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "item",
          "volumeDb"
        ],
        "additionalProperties": false
      }
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "levels"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"audio_mix","arguments":{"levels":[]}}`

### `audio_analyze`

Loudness analysis. With a timeline item: Resolve's AnalyzeAudioLevels (integrated LUFS + true peak, Resolve 21+). With a file path: FFmpeg EBU R128 (LUFS, LRA, true peak) plus silence ranges.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve API + FFmpeg / files

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "itemId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "trackType": {
      "const": "audio",
      "default": "audio"
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "itemIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "path": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 3,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Absolute local file path"
    },
    "standard": {
      "type": "string",
      "default": "EBU R128"
    },
    "silenceThresholdDb": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": -90,
      "maximum": 0,
      "default": -35
    },
    "minSilenceSec": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0.1,
      "maximum": 30,
      "default": 0.6
    },
    "targetLufs": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": -40,
      "maximum": -5,
      "default": -14
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"audio_analyze","arguments":{}}`

### `ai_analyze_footage`

```json
  "type": "object",
```
