```json
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 1024,
        "description": "Media pool clip id, exact clip name, or file path"
      }
    },
    "bin": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "deep": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "limit": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 500,
      "default": 100
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"ai_analyze_footage","arguments":{}}`

### `ai_detect_scenes`

Detect scene cuts. source 'timeline' runs Resolve's DetectSceneCuts on the current timeline (adds cuts); source 'file' runs FFmpeg scene detection on a clip's source file and returns cut times (no changes).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve API + FFmpeg / files

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "source": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "file",
        "timeline"
      ],
      "default": "file"
    },
    "clip": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Media pool clip id, exact clip name, or file path"
    },
    "threshold": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0.05,
      "maximum": 0.95,
      "default": 0.35
    },
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"ai_detect_scenes","arguments":{}}`

### `ai_detect_speakers`

Identify speakers in dialogue. (Not scriptable: Resolve's speaker detection is not scriptable and no diarization provider adapter is configured.)

- **Status:** unsupported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Implementation:** unsupported (returns `UNSUPPORTED` with an alternative)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "clip": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Media pool clip id, exact clip name, or file path"
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"ai_detect_speakers","arguments":{}}`

### `ai_detect_objects`

Prepare clips for object/face search: runs IntelliSearch analysis (MediaPoolItem.AnalyzeForIntellisearch, Resolve Studio 21+). Searching the analysis by phrase (e.g. 'person in a red shirt') happens in the Media page search bar: the query itself is not scriptable.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "clips": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 1024,
        "description": "Media pool clip id, exact clip name, or file path"
      }
    },
    "identifyFaces": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "better": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "clips"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"ai_detect_objects","arguments":{"clips":[]}}`

### `ai_search_media`

Natural-language-ish media search: matches every word against clip names, file paths, metadata and saved transcripts (from ai_transcribe_audio). Ranked by hits.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Implementation:** server-side planner / workflow

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "query": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1
    },
    "limit": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 200,
      "default": 25
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "query"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"ai_search_media","arguments":{"query":"Example"}}`

### `ai_transcribe_audio`

Transcribe a clip. engine 'resolve' uses Resolve's built-in transcription (Studio). engine 'provider' sends the source file to the configured AI provider and saves transcript JSON + SRT under output/transcripts.

- **Status:** requires_provider
- **Kind:** external service
- **Implementation:** external AI provider

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "clip": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Media pool clip id, exact clip name, or file path"
    },
    "engine": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "resolve",
        "provider"
      ],
      "default": "resolve"
    },
    "language": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "clip"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"ai_transcribe_audio","arguments":{"clip":"Example"}}`

### `ai_generate_subtitles`

Generate timeline subtitles (Resolve Studio auto-captions). For provider-based captions use subtitle_generate with engine 'provider'.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** server-side planner / workflow

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "language": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "charsPerLine": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 60
    },
    "lines": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "const": 1
        },
        {
          "const": 2
        }
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"ai_generate_subtitles","arguments":{}}`

### `ai_generate_voiceover`

Generate a voiceover. engine 'resolve' (default) uses Resolve's AI Speech Generator (Project.GenerateSpeech, Studio 21+ with the Extra installed; max 350 characters, voices like 'Female 1', 'Male 1'). engine 'provider' uses the configured TTS provider, imports the WAV into 'Voiceover' and can place it.

- **Status:** requires_provider
- **Kind:** external service
- **Implementation:** external AI provider

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "text": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 4000
    },
    "engine": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "resolve",
        "provider"
      ],
      "default": "resolve"
    },
    "voice": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "speed": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": -10,
      "maximum": 10
    },
    "pitch": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": -2,
      "maximum": 2
    },
    "fileName": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[\\w-]+$",
      "default": "voiceover"
    },
    "place": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "default": 0
    },
    "recordFrame": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "timecode": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "text"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"ai_generate_voiceover","arguments":{"text":"Example"}}`

### `ai_create_editing_plan`

Build a reviewable edit plan (ordered segments with source ranges, titles, music, look, export) from the media pool. Makes no changes; review then apply with ai_create_rough_cut { plan }.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Implementation:** server-side planner / workflow

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "clips": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 1024,
        "description": "Media pool clip id, exact clip name, or file path"
      }
    },
    "bin": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "goal": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 500,
      "description": "What to make, e.g. '60-second YouTube ad for a coffee brand'"
    },
    "targetSeconds": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 3,
      "maximum": 7200,
      "default": 60
    },
    "shotSeconds": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0.5,
      "maximum": 60,
      "default": 4
    },
    "keywords": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string"
      }
    },
    "musicClip": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Media pool clip id, exact clip name, or file path"
    },
    "title": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "look": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "exportPreset": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "fps": {
      "type": "number"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "goal"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"ai_create_editing_plan","arguments":{"goal":"Example"}}`

Rank clips by usefulness (resolution, length, Good Take / rating metadata, naming, keyword matches) with reasons.

```json
{

    },

      "type": "array",
```

### `ai_create_rough_cut`

Create a NEW timeline from an edit plan (from ai_create_editing_plan) or build a plan on the fly. Existing timelines are never modified. apply: false returns only the plan.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** server-side planner / workflow

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "clips": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 1024,
        "description": "Media pool clip id, exact clip name, or file path"
      }
    },
    "bin": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "goal": {
      "type": "string",
      "default": "Rough cut"
    },
    "targetSeconds": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 3,
      "maximum": 7200,
      "default": 60
    },
    "shotSeconds": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0.5,
      "maximum": 60,
      "default": 4
    },
    "keywords": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string"
      }
    },
    "musicClip": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Media pool clip id, exact clip name, or file path"
    },
    "title": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "look": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "exportPreset": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "fps": {
      "type": "number"
    },
    "plan": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "goal": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "targetSeconds": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "fps": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "estimatedSeconds": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "segments": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "clipId": {
                "type": "string"
              },
              "clipName": {
                "type": "string"
              },
              "sourceStart": {
                "type": "integer"
              },
              "sourceEnd": {
                "type": "integer"
              },
              "role": {
                "type": "string"
              },
              "note": {
                "type": "string"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "clipId",
              "clipName",
              "sourceStart",
              "sourceEnd",
              "role"
            ],
            "additionalProperties": false
          }
        },
        "titles": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "text": {
                "type": "string"
              },
              "atSecond": {
                "type": "number"
              },
              "kind": {
                "type": "string"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "text",
              "atSecond",
              "kind"
            ],
            "additionalProperties": false
          },
          "default": []
        },
        "musicClipId": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "look": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "exportPreset": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "notes": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "default": []
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "goal",
        "targetSeconds",
        "fps",
        "estimatedSeconds",
        "segments"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "timelineName": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "default": "AI Rough Cut"
    },
    "apply": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"ai_create_rough_cut","arguments":{}}`

Social cut: plan + NEW timeline at the platform aspect ratio (9:16, 1:1, 4:5, 16:9). Optionally runs Smart Reframe (Studio) on placed items.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** server-side planner / workflow

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {

      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 1024,
        "description": "Media pool clip id, exact clip name, or file path"
      }
    },
    "bin": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "aspect": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "9:16",
        "1:1",
        "4:5",
        "16:9"
      ],
      "default": "9:16"
    },
    "targetSeconds": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 3,
      "maximum": 180,
      "default": 30
    },
    "shotSeconds": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0.5,
      "maximum": 10,
      "default": 2.5
    },
    "keywords": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string"
      }
    },
    "title": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "timelineName": {
      "type": "string",
      "default": "AI Social"
    },
    "smartReframe": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "apply": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

### `ai_remove_silence`

Remove silent gaps from a timeline item (dialogue): FFmpeg finds silences in the source range, the item (and its linked audio) is replaced by the kept ranges placed back-to-back. Run with apply: false first to review the cut list.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve API + FFmpeg / files

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "itemId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "trackType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "video",
        "audio",
        "subtitle"
      ],
      "default": "video"
    },
    "trackIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "itemIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "thresholdDb": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": -80,
      "maximum": -10,
      "default": -35
    },
    "minSilenceSec": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0.2,
      "maximum": 10,
      "default": 0.6
    },
    "padSec": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 1,
      "default": 0.15
    },
    "apply": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "confirm": {
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"ai_remove_silence","arguments":{}}`

```json
      ],
      "description": "Photo name, id or 1-based index in the album"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "photo"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"photo_get_info","arguments":{"photo":{}}}`

### `photo_edit`

Set any documented PhotoAlbumItem properties at once (Pan, Tilt, ZoomX/Y, RotationAngle, FlipX/Y, CropLeft/Right/Top/Bottom, CropRatio, Source* section toggles...). All keys are validated by Resolve together.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "album": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "photo": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string"
        },
        {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 1
        }
      ],
      "description": "Photo name, id or 1-based index in the album"
    },
    "properties": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {
        "anyOf": [
          {
            "type": "number"
          },
          {
            "type": "boolean"
          },
          {
            "type": "string"
          }
        ]
      }
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "photo",
    "properties"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"photo_edit","arguments":{"photo":{},"properties":{}}}`

### `photo_crop`

Crop / reframe a photo (pixels from each edge, optional zoom and rotation).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "album": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "photo": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string"
        },
        {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 1
        }
      ],
      "description": "Photo name, id or 1-based index in the album"
    },
    "left": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "default": 0
    },
    "right": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "default": 0
    },
    "top": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "default": 0
    },
    "bottom": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "default": 0
    },
    "lockAspect": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "zoom": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0.01,
      "maximum": 100
    },
    "rotation": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": -360,
      "maximum": 360
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "photo"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"photo_crop","arguments":{"photo":{}}}`

### `photo_set_cdl`

Primary correction on a photo via ASC CDL (slope/offset/power/saturation). A grade version is saved first.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "album": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "photo": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string"
        },
        {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 1
        }
      ],
      "description": "Photo name, id or 1-based index in the album"
    },
    "nodeIndex": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "default": 1
    },
    "slope": {
      "type": "array",
      "prefixItems": [
        {
          "type": "number"
        },
        {
          "type": "number"
        },
        {
          "type": "number"
        }
      ],
      "default": [
        1,
        1,
        1
      ]
    },
    "offset": {
      "type": "array",
      "prefixItems": [
        {
          "type": "number"
        },
        {
          "type": "number"
        },
        {
          "type": "number"
        }
      ],
      "default": [
        0,
        0,
        0
      ]
    },
    "power": {
      "type": "array",
      "prefixItems": [
        {
          "type": "number"
        },
        {
          "type": "number"
        },
        {
          "type": "number"
        }
      ],
      "default": [
        1,
        1,
        1
      ]
    },
    "saturation": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 4,
      "default": 1
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "photo"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

### `photo_export`

Export photos (all in the album, or selected) as finished images with PhotoAlbumItem.ExportFrame (jpg/png/tif), optional max dimension and JPEG quality. Never overwrites without overwrite: true.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "album": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "photos": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "anyOf": [
          {
            "type": "string"
          },
          {
            "type": "integer"
          }
        ]
      }
    },
    "outputDir": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 3,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Absolute local file path"
    },
    "format": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "jpg",
        "png",
        "tif"
      ],
      "default": "jpg"
    },
    "prefix": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[\\w-]*$",
      "default": ""
    },
    "maxDimension": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 16,
      "maximum": 30000
    },
    "quality": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 100
    },
    "overwrite": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "outputDir"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"photo_export","arguments":{"outputDir":"C:\\Users\\you\\Videos\\example.mp4"}}`

### `photo_from_media_pool`

Create (or add to) a photo album from still images already in the media pool.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "album": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1
    },
    "photos": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 1024,
        "description": "Media pool clip id, exact clip name, or file path"
      }
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "album",
    "photos"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"photo_from_media_pool","arguments":{"album":"Example","photos":[]}}`

## 15. Rendering

### `render_get_settings`

Current render format/codec, render mode, saved render presets and (for the current codec) supported resolutions.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {},
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"render_get_settings","arguments":{}}`

### `render_list_formats`

Render formats (and optionally codecs per format) this Resolve supports.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "withCodecs": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"render_list_formats","arguments":{}}`

### `render_list_presets`

Built-in MCP export presets (YouTube, TikTok, Reels, Stories, Feed, Facebook, Master, Archive, sequences, audio).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {},
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"render_list_presets","arguments":{}}`

### `render_set_settings`

Set render format/codec (validated) and/or raw render settings (TargetDir, CustomName, FormatWidth/Height, FrameRate, VideoQuality, ExportAudio/Video, MarkIn/Out, AudioCodec, AudioBitDepth, AudioSampleRate, ...). Or load a saved Resolve render preset.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "format": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "codec": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "settings": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {
        "anyOf": [
          {
            "type": "string"
          },
          {
            "type": "number"
          },
          {
            "type": "boolean"
          }
        ]
      }
    },
    "resolvePreset": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "mode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "individual",
        "single"
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"render_set_settings","arguments":{}}`

### `render_add_job`

Configure a render from an export preset (format/codec validated) and add it to the render queue without starting.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "preset": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "youtube_1080p",
        "youtube_4k",
        "tiktok",
        "instagram_reels",
        "instagram_stories",
        "instagram_feed",
        "facebook",
        "professional_master",
        "archive",
        "png_sequence",
        "tiff_sequence",
        "exr_sequence",
        "audio_wav",
        "custom"
      ],
      "default": "youtube_1080p"
    },
    "outputDir": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 3,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Absolute local file path"
    },
    "fileName": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 200,
      "pattern": "^[^\\\\/:*?\"<>|]+$",
      "description": "File name without extension"
    },
    "format": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "codec": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "width": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 16,
      "maximum": 16384
    },
    "height": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 16,
      "maximum": 16384
    },
    "frameRate": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "quality": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "integer"
        },
        {
          "type": "string"
        }
      ]
    },
    "markIn": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "markOut": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "exportAudio": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "exportVideo": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "overwrite": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "outputDir",
    "fileName"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"render_add_job","arguments":{"outputDir":"C:\\Users\\you\\Videos\\example.mp4","fileName":"C:\\Users\\you\\Videos\\example.mp4"}}`

### `render_start`

Start rendering queued jobs (all, or the given ids). Returns immediately; poll render_get_status.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "jobIds": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string"
      }
    },
    "interactive": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "background": {
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"render_start","arguments":{}}`

```json

  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

```json
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Remove a job from the render queue (all: true clears the queue). Rendered files are not deleted. DESTRUCTIVE: requires confirm: true after explicit user confirmation.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** destructive (confirm: true)
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "jobId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "all": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "confirm": {
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"render_remove_job","arguments":{}}`

### `render_export_timeline`

One-step export: configure from a preset, queue, render, wait (with progress notifications), then verify the output file (exists, size, ffprobe metadata). Never reports success without a verified file.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** Resolve scripting API

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "description": "Timeline name or id"
    },
    "preset": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "youtube_1080p",
        "youtube_4k",
        "tiktok",
        "instagram_reels",
        "instagram_stories",
        "instagram_feed",
        "facebook",
        "professional_master",
        "archive",
        "png_sequence",
        "tiff_sequence",
        "exr_sequence",
        "audio_wav",
```

## 16. Workflows

### `workflow_create_youtube_video`

YouTube production: project (1080p/4K), import + organize footage, analyze, rough cut from best clips, B-roll interleave, intro title + lower third, optional subtitles, grade, background music, render YouTube preset and verify. Reports every step; fails if a required step fails.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Implementation:** server-side planner / workflow

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "projectName": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 255
    },
    "footageFolder": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 3,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Absolute local file path"
    },
    "footage": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 3,
        "maxLength": 1024,
        "description": "Absolute local file path"
      }
    },
    "musicFile": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 3,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Absolute local file path"
    },
    "title": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 500
    },
    "targetSeconds": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 3,
      "maximum": 7200,
      "default": 60
    },
    "look": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "hollywood_cinematic",
        "teal_orange",
        "filmic_warm",
        "vintage_film",
        "black_and_white",
        "moody_cinematic",
        "bright_commercial",
        "natural_documentary",
        "desaturated_cinematic",
        "night_cinematic"
      ]
    },
    "render": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "exportPreset": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "youtube_1080p",
        "youtube_4k",
        "tiktok",
        "instagram_reels",
        "instagram_stories",
        "instagram_feed",
        "facebook",
        "professional_master",
        "archive",
        "png_sequence",
        "tiff_sequence",
        "exr_sequence",
        "audio_wav",
        "custom"
      ]
    },
    "outputDir": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 3,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Absolute local file path"
    },
    "generateSubtitles": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "dryRun": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "resolution": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "1080p",
        "4k"
      ],
      "default": "1080p"
    },
    "frameRate": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "23.976",
        "24",
        "25",
        "29.97",
        "30",
        "50",
        "59.94",
        "60"
      ],
      "default": "24"
    },
    "lowerThird": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "musicLevelDb": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": -60,
      "maximum": 0,
      "default": -18
    },
    "background": {
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"workflow_create_youtube_video","arguments":{}}`
